Entry 1
U: I'm undecided on what event I want to attend next. Name some off for me and I'll decide on one.
A: Are there any specific events or locations that you are looking for?
U: I'd be interested in attending the Golden Bears Vs Trojans events. It's an anticipated match-up. As far as location is concerned, I'm looking for something in Berkeley.
A: The Golden Bears Vs Trojans event will take place on March 2nd at the California Memorial Stadium.
U: Sounds good, but I was wondering if there are any other events. What about Jordan Rakei? Will they be performing in LAX anytime soon?
A: There is an event on 9 days in the near future. On March 1st, Jordan Rakei will be at the Regent Theater DTLA.
U: Tell me what the start time is.
A: The scheduled event will begin at 5 pm.
U: Tell me where the Regent Theater DTLA is located.
A: The Regent Theater is located at 448 South Main Street.
U: I'm not sure if that day will work. What about other days for that event?
A: Another day for Jordan Rakei is March 4th at the Regent Theater DTLA.
U: That sounds like it would fit my schedule better.
A: Do you want tickets for the event on this date?
U: No I don't think I'll purchase those just yet.
A: Can I assist you with anything else today?
U: I'll need transportation there. Can you find me tickets for the bus with a departure time of 7:15 in the morning?
A: Where will you be departing from?
U: I will be departing from San Francisco that day.
A: What day would you like to leave at 7:15? Is March 4th an acceptable date?
U: That won't work for me. I am planning to make the trip later today.
A: How many tickets will you be needing then?
U: Please purchase 1 ticket for me.
A: Concerning your trip, you want to depart San Francisco to head to Los Angeles on March 1st at 7:15 am. If so, I will purchase 1 Economy class ticket for you.
U: Yes that works just fine for me.
A: I was not able to complete that transaction. Would a bus at 7 am for $50 be acceptable?
U: Not what I had in mind but it will have to do.
A: Your reservation has been confirmed.
U: Okay back to the event. Please purchase two tickets for the event for me.
A: Confirm the following. You want 2 tickets for Jordan Rakei on March 4th in Los Angeles?
U: Yes that information is correct.
A: I have booked your tickets and the reservation is confirmed.
U: What's the location of the venue again?
A: The event will be taking place at 448 South Main Street.
U: Okay thank you for the help.
A: Do you need help with anything else?
U: No thank you. I appreciate the help you provided.
A: You are very welcome. Have a good day.

Entry 2
U: With our vacation coming up, we want to set up something in advance this time, like some entertaining event. What can you come up with for us, please?

U: Well, the word is that the Blue Jays Vs Yankees game in Toronto, Ontario, is going to be a blast.

A: The start time is 5 pm at the 1 Blue Jays Way address.
U: Okay, now take a look at some other dates, please. How about for The Struts, perhaps in San Diego?

U: Let's hold off for a bit and check on bus tickets to get there, first.

A: All right. If March 5th would be your bus departure date, what's the time you want to leave?
U: Leaving at 12:45 on next Monday would be great.
A: Okay, and for how many passengers?

A: Got it. I'm setting up a purchase of 4 Economy tickets, leaving Sacramento at 12:45 pm on March 4th and arriving in San Diego. Are you ready to confirm that?

A: Your bus tickets are all set for your trip.
U: Many thanks. Now, put me in touch with getting tickets for the event, please.
A: Sure. Would you like to confirm 4 tickets to see The Struts on March 5th in San Diego?
U: Yes, I would, for sure.
A: Your ticket reservation is now confirmed. Enjoy the event.
U: Thanks. When and where will it take place?
A: The start time will be 6:30 pm at the street address 2891 University Avenue.
U: All right, we're done, and you rock.
A: Enjoy an awesome day.
